2026

International Conference on Modern Trends in Inorganic Chemistry (MTIC-2027) will be hosted by the Department of Chemistry, IIT Kanpur, after a gap of 30 years. The conference was last held at IIT Kanpur in 1997.
December 7, 2027 (Tuesday): Welcome dinner and evening lecture
December 8–11, 2027 (Wednesday–Saturday): Four Full-day scientific programme

The Chemical Society, IIT Kanpur is going to organize the Freshers’ Welcome Programme 2026 for the newly admitted UG and PG students of the Department of Chemistry. The programme will be held on Monday, 12 October 2026, from 6:00 PM onwards in the Main Auditorium, IIT Kanpur, followed by dinner on the adjacent lawn.

=== dr rajendra rathore colloquium of chemistry ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/chm/dr-rajendra-rathore-colloquium-of-chemistry
"Dr. Rajendra Rathore Colloquium of Chemistry" seminar series has been established at the Department of Chemistry, IIT Kanpur, through an endowment fund created through generous contributions from Mrs. Rajni Nigam, wife of late Dr. Rajendra Rathore, and their family members and well-wishers.

Dr. Rajendra Rathore, Pfletschinger-Habermann Professor of Organic Chemistry at Marquette University, Milwaukee, Wisconsin USA, departed this world on February 16, 2018, after a short but strenuous battle with pulmonary disease. Dr. Rathore was an alumnus of Indian Institute of Technology Kanpur who received his M.Sc. degree in Chemistry in 1986. Dr. Rathore was a scientist of the highest calibre. A pioneer in the field of organic and synthetic chemistry, he made key contributions to the areas of organic supramolecular and materials chemistry.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > dr rajendra rathore colloquium of chemistry]

Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/chm/latest-news
2026

Prof. Nisanth N. Nair has been elected as a fellow of the National Academy of Sciences.

Prof. Sandeep Verma has been elected as a fellow of the National Academy of Medical Sciences in 2026 in the field of biomedical engineering. He has also accepted a formal invitation to join the Publishing Board (PUB) of the Royal Society of Chemistry and its Science & Publications Sub-Committee (PUB S & P). PUB is the management board for the Royal Society of Chemistry's publishing business.

Mr. Manojkumar, (22107274), a PhD student in the group of Dr. Srinivas Dharavath, has received the Chemistry for Sustainability: Emerging Researcher Award at the RSC India Sustainability Forum 2026, organized by the Royal Society of Chemistry and held at the CSIR-National Chemical Laboratory, Pune, in collaboration with the INFLIBNET Centre, on 9th September 2026.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > latest news]
Prof. J. N. Moorthy has been invited to receive an honorary degree (honoris causa) by the University of Würzburg, Germany.

Dr. Vishal G. Rao and Dr. Ritika Gautam Singh have been awarded Excellence-in-Teaching Award (ETA) of IIT Kanpur for this year.

Prof. S. P. Rath has been awarded the National award for teachers in higher education institutions and polytechnics (National Award to Teachers of HEIs & Polytechnics - 2026).

Dr. P. C. Mondal has been awarded the JSPS invitational fellowship for research in Japan (short term), FY 2026.

Dr. Anantharaj Sengeni, who has been awarded the G. D. Naidu Young Scientist Award 2026 by the People's Thinking Forum, Tamil Nadu.

Dr. Manabendra Chandra from the Department of Chemistry has been selected to receive the Chemical Research Society of India (CRSI) Bronze Medal for the year 2026.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > latest news]
Dr. Ritika Gautam Singh from the Department of Chemistry has been selected to receive the Darshan Ranganathan Memorial Lecture Award 2027, by the Chemical Research Society of India (CRSI).

Dr. Anantharaj Sengeni has been selected for the electrochemical society of India associate fellowship 2026.

Dr. Vishal Govind Rao has been conferred with the NASI Young Scientist (NYS)–2026 award by the National Academy of Sciences, India (NASI) in recognition of his scientific contributions and research potential.

Prof. Basker Sunderaraju has been conferred with the N. C. Nigam Chair with effect from 9 May 2026 for a period of 3 years.

Prof. Manabendra Chandra has been conferred the Ranjit Singh Chair with effect from 9 May 2026 for a period of three years.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > latest news]
Prof. Nisanth N Nair has joined the editorial advisory board of the Journal of Physical Chemistry Letters from January 2026 for a period of 3 years.

2025

Ms. Parkhi Sharma (20107288), a Ph.D. student in the group of Dr. Apparao Draksharapu, has received Best Poster Presentation Award in Modern Trends in Inorganic Chemistry (MTIC-XXII), held at University of Delhi, from Dec 18-21, 2025.

Ms. Farha Naaz (19107276), a Ph.D. student in the group of Dr. D.L.V.K. Prasad, has received Best Oral Presentation Award in the Institute Research Symposium (IRS 2025), held at IIT Kanpur, from Nov 7-9, 2025.

Dr. Ritika Gautam-Singh has been awared Merck Young Scientist Award 2025 – Season 4 Runner-Up in the Scientific Excellence category under Chemical Sciences. The award ceremony was held on 28th November 2025 in Bengaluru.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > latest news]
Ms. Neha Kumari, a PhD student of Raja Angamuthu, received the best poster award at the 10th Asian Conference on Coordination Chemistry (ACCC10) held in Hanoi, Vietnam from 24-27th October, 2025.

=== prof n sathyamurthy endowment lecture series ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/chm/prof-n-sathyamurthy-endowment-lecture-series
"Prof. N. Sathyamurthy Endowment Lecture Series" has been established at the Department of Chemistry, IIT Kanpur in 2024 through an endowment fund created through generous contributions by the students, collaborators, family, friends and well-wishers of Professor N. Sathyamurthy. With this endowment lecture series, this group of people wish to celebrate his sustained contributions to the advancement of theoretical and computational chemistry in India, while acknowledging the pioneering role of IIT Kanpur in the development of that vision.

Professor N. Sathyamurthy completed his M.Sc. from Annamalai University, Tamil Nadu, India (1972) and his Ph.D. from Oklahoma State University, USA (1975). Later, he joined the research group of Professor John C. Polanyi (Nobel Laureate, 1986), in the University of Toronto, Canada as a postdoctoral fellow (1975-78). Professor Sathyamurthy joined the Department of Chemistry, IIT Kanpur as a Lecturer in 1978, and rose to the rank of a Professor in 1985. He was made the Institute Chair Professor in 2007 and Institute Fellow in 2013. His tenure at IIT Kanpur was marked by aseries of notable achievements. He served the Institute in various capacities, including as the Head, Department of Chemistry and as the Dean of Faculty Affairs. To establish the Indian Institute of Science Education and Research (IISER) Mohali, he assumed charge as its Founder Director in 2007, and successfully completed the tenure for two consecutive terms. His tenure as Director at IISER Mohali saw its rise as one of the Institutes of Academic Excellence in India.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > prof n sathyamurthy endowment lecture series]

=== prof vinod k singh distinguished lecture series ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/chm/prof-vinod-k-singh-distinguished-lecture-series
"Prof. Vinod K. Singh Distinguished Lecture Series” was established at the Department of Chemistry, IIT Kanpur in 2024 through an endowment fund created through generous contributions by the students, collaborators, family, friends and well-wishers of Prof. Vinod K. Singh. The Distinguished Lecture Series aims to feature engaging speakers who present various perspectives on organic chemistry in an accessible and captivating manner. The lecture series is designed for a broad audience, encouraging participation from across the IIT Kanpur community. The lecture series is expected to cater to a wide range of researchers and graduate students in terms of exposure to the broad understanding of the scope of theoretical and computational chemistry, and for encouraging cutting-edge research in this area.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > prof vinod k singh distinguished lecture series]
Prof. Vinod K. Singh is a distinguished academician and leader in the field of Synthetic Organic Chemistry, with a career spanning over three decades. Currently, he is a Professor Emeritus at IITK, holding the Institute Chair position. He also holds the position of Director's Chair Professor at IISER Bhopal. Prof. Singh has made groundbreaking contributions to research and the management of higher educational institutions. His leadership extends beyond academia, with key roles in governing scientific research councils and national educational policies and mentoring future generations of scientists. Recognized globally for his expertise, Prof. Singh has been honored with prestigious awards like the Padma Shri (2014), Shanti Swarup Bhatnagar Prize (2004), Vigyan Ratna Award of U.P. (2006), J.C. Bose fellowship (2009), Samman Patra by UP Government (2018) reflecting his profound impact on science and education in India and beyond.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > prof vinod k singh distinguished lecture series]
Speaker: Prof. Varinder Aggarwal
Affiliation: University of Bristol, UK
Title: Synthesis with Boron at the Helm
Date: 14th of February at 17:00
Venue:  L-16
Tea: 16:30

=== recruiters ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/chm/recruiters
Students of Department of Chemistry are part of a potential workforce for industry, academia and government organizations. In addition to a deep knowledge of Chemistry, these students are exposed to other areas of science, engineering, humanities and social sciences, and are well-rounded individuals who can make significant contributions to society. If you are a recruiter interested in recruiting at the department, you can contact the Head of the Department (https://iitk.ac.in/chm/contact-us). The students of the department also participate in the institute placements and recruiters can also approach the Campus Placements Cell

=== seminar listing ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/chm/seminar-listing
Types of Seminars

Departmental Seminars: This includes seminars by visiting professors from other departments at IIT Kanpur and professors visiting IIT Kanpur from other institutes. These seminars are typically held in FB 426 at 4:30 pm, or at any other convenient time. The list of department seminars can be viewed at the link Click here.

PhD Open Seminar: This is an integral part of the PhD programme. Typically, every PhD student has to give an open seminar detailing his/her work a few months before submittiing their theses. These seminars are typically held in FB 426 at 4:30 pm or at any other convenient time. The list of department open seminars can be viewed at the link Click here.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > seminar listing]
Graduate Seminars: This is a seminar that every PhD student has to give to the department at the beginning of their PhD work. The list of department open seminars can be viewed at the link Click here.

Special Seminars: Every year, the Chemistry department organizes some special seminars under the Distinguished lecture series. More details of this series can be found at the link here.

Ph. D Defence Click here.

GIAN course by Prof. Martin Albrecht from University of Bern, Switzerland

Title: Sustainable Chemistry through Catalysis
Date: 21-24 March, 2017

Overview:To meet improved living standards of earth’s growing population, a constant supply of chemical products must be maintained. These chemicals need to be accessed in a simple and energy-efficient manner utilizing cheap and abundant natural sources with minimal chemical waste. Catalysis is the key technology to convert challenging substrates to useful chemicals. This course covers the fundamental and applied aspects of homogeneous catalysis. A specific focus will be directed on catalysis for oxidative transformations, and the ensuing aspects related to ligand design. Underlying design principles for ligands to operate under specific conditions will be discussed as well applications and perspectives, for example towards artifiicial photosynthesis and water splitting.

[Context: DEPARTMENT OF CHEMISTRY IIT KANPUR - SEMINARS, EVENTS, WORKSHOPS, NEWS AND ANNOUNCEMENTS > special seminar]
Further details about registration: see the link
For Course registration: Contact Dr. Sabuj Kundu and Jitendra Bera, Phone: 0512-2597336, E-mail: [email address hidden on the website]
